Model before rewriting:

letting a be {1,2,3,4}
find t: int(0..100)
find x: int(0..100)
find y: int(0..100)
find z: int(0..100)

such that

x = |a|,
y = min(a),
z = max(a),
allDifferent([x,y,z,t;int(1..4)]),
t in a
